add_namespace = occupation

country_event = {
	id = occupation.1
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.1"}
	title = "Occupation of Poland"
	desc = occupation.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = "Release Poland"
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = SOV
			}
			remove_state_claim = 97
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = POL
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = POL
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			POL = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = POL	
		POL = { 
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Release Poland and return eastern territories"
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				BLR = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				UKR = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = POL
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = POL
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			POL = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = POL	
		POL = { 
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		IF = {
			limit = { UKR = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } 
			UKR = { 
				transfer_state = 93
				transfer_state = 91
				transfer_state = 89
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = { BLR = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } 
			BLR = { 
				transfer_state = 94 
				transfer_state = 95 
				transfer_state = 96 
				transfer_state = 784
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = { 
				BLR = { is_subject_of = ROOT } 
				has_global_flag = road_to_56_is_active
			} 
			BLR = { 
				transfer_state = 935
				transfer_state = 936
			}			
		}		
	}
	option = {
		name = "Polish sovereignty was a mistake"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = POL_OCCUPIED
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = occupation.2
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.2"}
	title = "Occupation of Switzerland"
	desc = occupation.2.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = "Give Switzerland to our Southern German ally"
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		trigger = {
			BAY = { is_ally_with = ROOT }
			50 = { is_owned_by = BAY }
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SWI
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SWI
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}				
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			BAY = { transfer_state = PREV }
			BAY = { add_state_core = PREV }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "A friendly regime will save lives."
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
				IF = {
					limit = {				
       						 ITA = {
           						 owns_state = 846
       						 }
   				 	}
					ITA = {
     						add_state_claim = 846
   				   	}
				}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SWI
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SWI
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			SWI = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = SWI
		SWI = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "The Alps are Ours!"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = SWI_OCCUPIED



	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = occupation.9
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.9"}
	title = "Occupation of Slovakia"
	desc = occupation.9.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = "Release Slovakia"
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
			owns_state = 70
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 70 }
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					owns_state = 70
					any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 70 }
				}
				70 = {
				NOT = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = ROOT
							is_claimed_by = ROOT
							}
						}
				}
			}
			SLO = { transfer_state = 70 }
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				owns_state = 71
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 71 }
				}
				71 = {
				NOT = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = ROOT
							is_claimed_by = ROOT
							}
						}
				}
			}
			SLO = { transfer_state = 71 }
		}
		puppet = SLO
		SLO = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = SLO
				}
			add_core_of = SLO
			}
	}


	option = {
		name = "Give it to Hungary"
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
			HUN = { exists = yes }
			OR = {
			owns_state = 70
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 70 }
			}
			HUN = { is_ally_with = ROOT }
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SLO
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SLO
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
			}
			HUN = { transfer_state = PREV }
			add_claim_by = HUN
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = SLO_OCCUPIED
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

			LUX = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = LUX
		LUX = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Integrate Luxembourg"
		trigger = {
			LUX = { exists = no }
			OR = {
				original_tag = FRA
				original_tag = HOL
			}
			any_owned_state = { 
				any_neighbor_state = { 
					is_core_of = LUX
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = LUX
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = LUX
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			ROOT = { add_state_claim = PREV }
		}
	}	
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = LUX_OCCUPIED



	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = occupation.36
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.36"}
	title = "Occupation of Croatia"
	desc = occupation.36.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = BOS
				}
				remove_core_of = CRO
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = road_to_56_is_active
				}
				CRO = { add_state_core = 952 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Release Croatia"
		trigger = {
			OR = {
			owns_state = 109
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 109 }
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = CRO
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
							NOT = { tag = ITA }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = CRO
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			CRO = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = CRO
		CRO = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = CRO
				}
			add_core_of = CRO
			}
        }
	option = {
		trigger = {
			YUG = { exists = yes }
			YUG = { is_ally_with = ROOT }
			OR = {
			owns_state = 109
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 109 }
			}
		}
		name = "Return Croatia to Yugoslavia"
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				owns_state = 109
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 109 }
				}
			}
			YUG = { transfer_state = 109 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = CRO_OCCUPIED
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Uruguay
	id = occupation.48
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.48"}
	title = "Occupation of Uruguay"
	desc = occupation.48.d

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = no
	option = {
		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = URG
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = URG
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			URG = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = URG
		URG = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = URG_OCCUPIED



	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

###occupation of Manchuria
country_event = {
	id = occupation.70
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.70"}
	title = "Occupation of Manchuria"
	desc = occupation.70.d
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				PRC = { exists = yes }
			}
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				PRC = { exists = no }
			}
			ROOT = { clr_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "Give it to PRC."
		trigger = {
			has_government = communism
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = MAN
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = MAN
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			PRC = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC }
			}
			puppet = PRC
			clr_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					is_dynamic_country = yes
					has_government = communism
					OR = {
						original_tag = PRC
						original_tag = CHI
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = dynamic_prc_target
				PRC = {
					annex_country = {
						target = event_target:dynamic_prc_target
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		trigger = {
			NOT = { 
				has_government = communism 
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				controls_state = 612
				612 = { owner = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } 
				}
			}
			MAN = { transfer_state = 612 }
			MAN = { add_state_claim = 612 }
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				controls_state = 611
				611 = { owner = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } 
				}
			}
			MAN = { transfer_state = 611 }
			MAN = { add_state_claim = 611 }
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				controls_state = 609
				609 = { owner = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } 
				}
			}
			MAN = { transfer_state = 609 }
			MAN = { add_state_core = 609 }
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = MAN
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = MAN
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			MAN = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = MAN
		MAN = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = JAP
				RNG = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = MAN
					is_claimed_by = MAN
				}
			}
			remove_core_of = RNG
		}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = MAN_OCCUPIED

	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = occupation.74
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.74"}
	title = "Occupation of Tibet"
	desc = occupation.74.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = "Give it to PRC."
		trigger = {
			has_government = communism
			PRC = { exists = yes is_ally_with = ROOT }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = TIB 
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = TIB
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			PRC = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					has_government = communism
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				controls_state = 601
				601 = { owner = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } 
				}
			}
			TIB = { transfer_state = 601 }
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = TIB
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = TIB
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			TIB = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = TIB
		TIB = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = TIB
					is_claimed_by = TIB
				}
			}
			remove_core_of = RNG
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = TIB_OCCUPIED



	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

	id = occupation.112
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event occupation.112"}
	title = "Occupation of Togoland"
	desc = occupation.112.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		TOG = {
			add_state_core = 777
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = TOG
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = TOG
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			TOG = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = TOG
		TOG = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}

	}
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = TOG_OCCUPIED



	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		UZB = {
			add_state_core = 405
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = UZB
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = UZB
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			UZB = { transfer_state = PREV }

		}
		puppet = UZB
		UZB = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}

			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = UZB
				}
				remove_core_of = SOV
				remove_claim_by = SOV
			}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = UZB_OCCUPIED



	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

			}
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC }

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				PRC = { exists = no }
			}
			ROOT = { clr_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Transfer it to PRC."
		trigger = {
			has_government = communism
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = CHI
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = CHI
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			PRC = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC }
			}
			puppet = PRC
			clr_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = dontpuppetPRC
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					is_dynamic_country = yes
					has_government = communism
					OR = {
						original_tag = PRC
						original_tag = CHI
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = dynamic_prc_target
				PRC = {
					annex_country = {
						target = event_target:dynamic_prc_target
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		trigger = {
			NOT = { 
				has_government = communism 
			}
			NOT = { tag = JAP }
		}
		CHI = {
			add_state_core = 613
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = CHI
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = CHI
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			CHI = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = CHI
		CHI = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				601 = { OR = { is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT owner = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } }
				TIB = { exists = yes }
			}
			TIB = { transfer_state = 601 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Install a collaborationist regime."
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
		trigger = {
			original_tag = JAP
			NOT = { has_government = communism }
		}
		RNG = {
			add_state_core = 613
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = CHI
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = CHI
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			RNG = { transfer_state = PREV }
			add_core_of = RNG
		}
		puppet = RNG
		RNG = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				601 = { OR = { is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT owner = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } }
				TIB = { exists = yes }
			}
			TIB = { transfer_state = 601 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Maintain Military occupation."
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
		set_country_flag = CHI_OCCUPIED
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

		}

			NOT = { has_government = communism }
		}
			ai_chance = {
				base = 0
				factor = 0

	id = fra.partition.1
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event fra.partition.1"}
	title = ""
	desc = fra.partition.1.d
	
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		owns_state = 16
		FRA = { exists = no }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = conquest_FRA }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = nocontrol_FRA }
	}
	fire_only_once = no
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = { flag = fra_state_count value = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = FRA
				is_on_continent = europe
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
            }
			ROOT = { 
				modify_country_flag = { flag = fra_state_count value = 1 } 
				set_country_flag = conquest_FRA
			}
        }
		IF = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = conquest_FRA }
			}
			set_country_flag = nocontrol_FRA
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "Trigger has fired"
	}
}

country_event = {			# - Actual Partition Event
	id = fra.partition.2
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event fra.partition.2"}
	title = "The End of France"
	desc = fra.partition.2.d
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	
	# - Generic Options
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = ALG
				}
				remove_core_of = FRA
			}
		}
	}	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = { flag = fra_state_count value > 0 }
		}
		name = "Liberate France"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = FRA
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
							NOT = { tag = RKB }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = FRA
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			FRA = {
				transfer_state = PREV
				add_state_core = PREV
			}
			IF = { 
				limit = {
					FRA = { is_puppet = no }
					}
				ROOT = { puppet = FRA }
			}
			FRA = {
				if = { 
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }

					load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				remove_all_claims = yes
				add_state_core = 16

				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 15 }
				}
		}

				OR = {
				owns_state = 14
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 14 }
				}
			}

				add_state_core = 14
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = BRI
					OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = BRI
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
				}

					transfer_state = PREV

		set_country_flag = decided_ALR

		name = "Do nothing for now"

	}

		name = "Release Slovenia"
		trigger = {
			OR = {
			owns_state = 102
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 102 }
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SLV
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
							NOT = { tag = ITA }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SLV
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
			}
			SLV = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		puppet = SLV
		SLV = {
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
				}
				load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
			}
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			remove_all_claims = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = SLV
				}
			add_core_of = SLV
			}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			CRO = { exists = no }
			YUG = { exists = yes }
			YUG = { is_ally_with = ROOT }
			OR = {
			owns_state = 102
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 102 }
			}
		}
		name = "Return Slovenia to Yugoslavia"
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SLV
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SLV
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
			}
			YUG = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			AUS = { exists = yes }
			AUS = { is_puppet_of = ROOT }
			OR = {
			owns_state = 102
			any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 102 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		name = "Give it to Austria"
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SLV
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SLV
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
			}
			AUS = { transfer_state = PREV }
			add_claim_by = AUS
		}
	}

add_namespace = uk.partition

country_event = {			# - Hidden Event which determines who has control over British land after Britain no longer exists
	id = uk.partition.1
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event uk.partition.1"}
	title = ""
	desc = uk.partition.1.d
	
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		owns_state = 126
		ENG = { exists = no }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = conquest_UK }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = nocontrol_UK }
	}
	fire_only_once = no
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = { flag = uk_state_count value = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ENG
				is_on_continent = europe
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
            }
			ROOT = { 
				modify_country_flag = { flag = uk_state_count value = 1 } 
				set_country_flag = conquest_UK
			}
        }
		IF = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = conquest_UK }
			}
			set_country_flag = nocontrol_UK
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "Trigger has fired"
	}
}

country_event = {			# - Actual Partition Event
	id = uk.partition.2
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event uk.partition.2"}
	title = "The End of the United Kingdom"
	desc = uk.partition.2.d
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	
	# - Generic Options
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = { flag = uk_state_count value > 0 }
		}
		name = "Liberate Great Britain"
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ENG
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = ENG
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			ENG = {
				transfer_state = PREV
				add_state_core = PREV
			}
			IF = { 
				limit = {
					ENG = { is_puppet = no }
					}
			ROOT = { puppet = ENG }
			}
			ENG = {
				if = { 
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
					}
					load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				remove_all_claims = yes	
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
				OR = {
				owns_state = 126
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 126 }
				}
				OR = {
				owns_state = 121
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 121 }
				}
				OR = {
				owns_state = 122
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 122 }
				}
		}
		name = "Break up the United Kingdom"
		every_state = {
				limit = {
					AND = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = SCO
							is_core_of = WLS
							is_core_of = IRE
						}
						OR = {
							is_core_of = ENG
							is_claimed_by = ENG
						}
					}
				}
				remove_core_of = ENG
				remove_claim_by = ENG
			}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				owns_state = 126
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 126 }
				}
			}
			release_puppet = ENG
			ENG = {
				if = { 
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
					}
					load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				remove_all_claims = yes
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				owns_state = 122
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 122 }
				}
			}
			WLS = { 
				add_state_core = 122
			}
			release_puppet = WLS
			WLS = {
				if = { 
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
					}
					load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				remove_all_claims = yes	
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
				owns_state = 121
				any_country = { is_subject_of = ROOT owns_state = 121 }
				}
			}
			SCO = { 
				add_state_core = 121
			}
			release_puppet = SCO
			SCO = {
				if = { 
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
					}
					load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				remove_all_claims = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "Maintain military occupation"
		set_country_flag = decided_UK
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
						}
						NOT = {
							owner = {
								capital_scope = {
									is_core_of = SHL
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
			}
			PRE = { transfer_state = PREV }
			PRE = { add_state_core = PREV }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "Maintain Occupation"
		set_country_flag = decided_SHL
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}

##############################
###SOVIET OCCUPATION EVENTS###
##############################

add_namespace = sov.partition

country_event = {			# - Hidden Event which determines who has control over Russian land after Soviet union no longer exists
	id = sov.partition.1
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event sov.partition.1"}
	title = ""
	desc = sov.partition.1.d
	
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		owns_state = 219
		SOV = { exists = no }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = conquest_SOV }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = nocontrol_SOV }
	}
	fire_only_once = no
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = { flag = sov_state_count value = 0 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = SOV
				is_on_continent = europe
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
            }
			ROOT = { 
				modify_country_flag = { flag = sov_state_count value = 1 } 
				set_country_flag = conquest_SOV
			}
        }
		IF = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = conquest_SOV }
			}
			set_country_flag = nocontrol_SOV
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "Trigger has fired"
	}
}

country_event = {			# - Actual Partition Event
	id = sov.partition.2
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event sov.partition.2"}
	title = "The End of the Soviet Union"
	desc = sov.partition.2.d
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	
	immediate = {
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_claimed_by = SOV
			}
			remove_claim_by = SOV
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		name = "Liberate Russia"
	every_state = {
		limit = {
				is_core_of = SOV
				OR = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					AND = {
						NOT = {
							AND = {
								state = 329
								329 = { 
									owner = {
										OR = {
											tag = MEN
											tag = MON
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						owner = { 
							is_subject_of = ROOT 
							NOT = { is_colony_or_reickskommissariat = yes }
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									tag = UKR
									tag = BLR
									tag = POL
									tag = LAT
									tag = EST
									tag = LIT
									tag = KAZ
									tag = TMS
									tag = UZB
									tag = KYR
									tag = TAJ
									tag = ARM
									tag = GEO
									tag = AZR
									tag = SIB
									tag = FIN
								}
							}
						}
						NOT = {
										owner = {
											capital_scope = {
												is_core_of = SOV
											}
										}
						}
						NOT = {
								state = 329
								329 = { 
									owner = {
										OR = {
											tag = MEN
											tag = MON
										}
									}
								}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = dec_later }
		}
		SOV = { transfer_state = PREV }
	}
	IF = { 
			limit = {
				SOV = { is_puppet = no }
			}
			ROOT = { puppet = SOV }
			}
			SOV = {
				if = { 
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = { rule = RULE_LOAD_GENERIC_FOCUS_PUPPET option = Disable }
					}
					load_focus_tree = generic_focus
				remove_all_ideas = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				remove_all_claims = yes
			}

	}

	option = {
		name = "Maintain military occupation"
		set_country_flag = SOV_OCCUPIED
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
	option = {
		name = "Do nothing for now"
		trigger = { is_ai = no }
	}
}
